c7 is a light square. d4 is a dark square.
No.

    If K is on f1, and knight moves to f2, it IS a check.
    So g1f1 might walk into a check from the knight on d1 (if it moves to f2).
    Therefore, g1h1 is safer.

    c3c4: pushes the pawn.
    h2h3/h2h4: pushes the pawn.
    g2g3/g2g4: pushes the pawn.
    These moves don't remove the pin.

    So, g1h1 seems like the best logical move to remove the pin and avoid immediate knight checks.
